1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the optics structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 740. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 657 and converges at rate O(1/n^3).

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the optics decomposition. By minimality, the element a^740 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 657-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

Theorem 214.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Theorem 214.3 (Optimality Condition)

Each subproblem has a solution by the optimality condition fixed-point theorem with constant 3. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
